An "ok" response would be acceptable, too, as long as a second FIN is not emitted (the first FIN may be retransmitted, though).

CLOSE-WAIT 状态

CLOSE-WAIT STATE

  • 将此请求排队，直到前面的所有 SEND 都已分段；然后发送 FIN 报文段，进入LAST-ACK 状态。Queue this request until all preceding SENDs have been segmentized; then send a FIN segment, enter LAST-ACK state.

CLOSING 状态

CLOSING STATE

LAST-ACK 状态

LAST-ACK STATE

TIME-WAIT 状态

TIME-WAIT STATE

  • 响应“错误：连接正在关闭”。Respond with "error: connection closing".

3.10.5. ABORT 调用

CLOSED 状态（即 TCB 不存在）

CLOSED STATE (i.e., TCB does not exist)

  • 如果用户不应访问此类连接，则返回“错误：此进程的连接非法”。If the user should not have access to such a connection, return "error: connection illegal for this process".
  • 否则返回“错误：连接不存在”。Otherwise, return "error: connection does not exist".

LISTEN 状态

LISTEN STATE

  • 任何未完成的 RECEIVE 都应返回“错误：连接复位”响应。删除 TCB，进入CLOSED状态，然后返回。Any outstanding RECEIVEs should be returned with "error: connection reset" responses. Delete TCB, enter CLOSED state, and return.

SYN-SENT 状态

SYN-SENT STATE

  • 所有排队的 SEND 和 RECEIVE 都应收到“连接复位”通知。删除 TCB，进入CLOSED状态，然后返回。All queued SENDs and RECEIVEs should be given "connection reset" notification. Delete the TCB, enter CLOSED state, and return.

CLOSE-WAIT 状态

  • 发送如下复位报文段：

    Send a reset segment:

    <SEQ=SND.NXT><CTL=RST>

    <SEQ=SND.NXT><CTL=RST>

  • 所有排队的 SEND 和 RECEIVE 都应收到“连接复位”通知；所有排队等待传输（除了上面形成的 RST）或重传的报文段都应当被清空。删除 TCB，进入CLOSED状态，然后返回。All queued SENDs and RECEIVEs should be given "connection reset" notification; all segments queued for transmission (except for the RST formed above) or retransmission should be flushed. Delete the TCB, enter CLOSED state, and return.

3.10.7. 报文段到达

3.10.7.1. CLOSED 状态

如果状态为 CLOSED（即 TCB 不存在），则

If the state is CLOSED (i.e., TCB does not exist), then

  • 丢弃传入报文段中的全部数据。若报文段含 RST，则直接丢弃；否则回送 RST。其确认字段和序号字段应选择适当值，使触发该复位的发送端能够接受它。all data in the incoming segment is discarded. An incoming segment containing a RST is discarded. An incoming segment not containing a RST causes a RST to be sent in response. The acknowledgment and sequence field values are selected to make the reset sequence acceptable to the TCP endpoint that sent the offending segment.

  • 如果 ACK 位关闭，则使用序号 0，

    If the ACK bit is off, sequence number zero is used,

    • <SEQ=0><ACK=SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN><CTL=RST,ACK><SEQ=0><ACK=SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN><CTL=RST,ACK>

  • 如果 ACK 位打开，

    If the ACK bit is on,

    • <SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST><SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST>
  • 返回。Return.
3.10.7.2. LISTEN 状态

如果状态为 LISTEN，则

If the state is LISTEN, then

  • 首先，检查 RST：

    First, check for a RST:

    • 传入的 RST 报文段可能无效，因为它无法响应此连接实体发送的任何内容而发送。应忽略传入的 RST。返回。An incoming RST segment could not be valid since it could not have been sent in response to anything sent by this incarnation of the connection. An incoming RST should be ignored. Return.

  • 其次，检查 ACK：

    Second, check for an ACK:

    • 如果任何确认到达仍处于 LISTEN 状态的连接，则都是错误的。应当为任何到达的承载 ACK 的报文段形成可接受的复位报文段。 RST 的格式应如下：

      Any acknowledgment is bad if it arrives on a connection still in the LISTEN state. An acceptable reset segment should be formed for any arriving ACK-bearing segment. The RST should be formatted as follows:

      • <SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST><SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST>
    • 返回。Return.

  • 第三，检查 SYN：

    Third, check for a SYN:

    • 如果设置了 SYN 位，请检查安全性。如果传入报文段上的安全/分区与 TCB 中的安全/分区不完全匹配，则发送复位并返回。

      If the SYN bit is set, check the security. If the security/compartment on the incoming segment does not exactly match the security/compartment in the TCB, then send a reset and return.

      • <SEQ=0><ACK=SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN><CTL=RST,ACK><SEQ=0><ACK=SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN><CTL=RST,ACK>

    • 设置 RCV.NXT = SEG.SEQ+1、IRS = SEG.SEQ；将其他控制信息或数据排队，留待后续处理。选择 ISS，并发送如下形式的 SYN 报文段：

      Set RCV.NXT to SEG.SEQ+1, IRS is set to SEG.SEQ, and any other control or text should be queued for processing later. ISS should be selected and a SYN segment sent of the form:

      • <SEQ=ISS><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=SYN,ACK><SEQ=ISS><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=SYN,ACK>
    • SND.NXT 设置为 ISS+1，SND.UNA 设置为 ISS。连接状态应更改为 SYN-RECEIVED。请注意，任何其他传入的控制或数据（与 SYN 组合）都将在 SYN-RECEIVED 状态下进行处理，但不应重复处理 SYN 和 ACK。如果未完全指定侦听（即未完全指定远程套接字），则现在应填写未指定的字段。SND.NXT is set to ISS+1 and SND.UNA to ISS. The connection state should be changed to SYN-RECEIVED. Note that any other incoming control or data (combined with SYN) will be processed in the SYN-RECEIVED state, but processing of SYN and ACK should not be repeated. If the listen was not fully specified (i.e., the remote socket was not fully specified), then the unspecified fields should be filled in now.

  • 第四，检查其他数据或控制信息：

    Fourth, other data or control:

    • 正常情况下不会到达此步骤。丢弃报文段并返回。任何其他不含 SYN 的控制或数据报文段都必须带 ACK，因此若未在第一步因 RST 被丢弃，就应已在第二步的 ACK 处理中被丢弃。This should not be reached. Drop the segment and return. Any other control or data-bearing segment (not containing SYN) must have an ACK and thus would have been discarded by the ACK processing in the second step, unless it was first discarded by RST checking in the first step.
3.10.7.3. SYN-SENT 状态

如果状态为 SYN-SENT，则

If the state is SYN-SENT, then

  • 首先，检查 ACK 位：

    First, check the ACK bit:

    • 如果设置了 ACK 位，

      If the ACK bit is set,

      • 如果 SEG.ACK =< ISS 或 SEG.ACK > SND.NXT，则发送复位（除非设置了 RST 位，如果设置了，则丢弃该报文段并返回）

        If SEG.ACK =< ISS or SEG.ACK > SND.NXT, send a reset (unless the RST bit is set, if so drop the segment and return)

        • <SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST><SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST>
      • 并丢弃该报文段。返回。and discard the segment. Return.
      • 如果 SND.UNA < SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT，则 ACK 可接受。一些部署的 TCP 代码使用了检查 SEG.ACK == SND.NXT（使用“==”而不是“=<”），但是当堆栈能够在 SYN 上发送数据时，这是不合适的，因为 TCP 对端可能无法接受和确认 SYN 上的所有数据。If SND.UNA < SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT, then the ACK is acceptable. Some deployed TCP code has used the check SEG.ACK == SND.NXT (using "==" rather than "=<"), but this is not appropriate when the stack is capable of sending data on the SYN because the TCP peer may not accept and acknowledge all of the data on the SYN.

  • 其次，检查 RST 位：

    Second, check the RST bit:

    • 如果设置了 RST 位，

      If the RST bit is set,

      • RFC 5961 [9] 中描述了潜在的盲复位攻击。该文件中描述的缓解措施具有其中解释的特定适用性，并且不能替代加密保护（例如 IPsec 或 TCP-AO）。支持 RFC 5961 中描述的缓解措施的 TCP 实现应当 (SHOULD) 在执行下一报文段中的操作之前首先检查序号是否与 RCV.NXT 完全匹配。A potential blind reset attack is described in RFC 5961 [9]. The mitigation described in that document has specific applicability explained therein, and is not a substitute for cryptographic protection (e.g., IPsec or TCP-AO). A TCP implementation that supports the mitigation described in RFC 5961 SHOULD first check that the sequence number exactly matches RCV.NXT prior to executing the action in the next paragraph.
      • 如果 ACK 可接受，则向用户发出信号“错误：连接复位”，丢弃该报文段，进入 CLOSED 状态，删除 TCB，然后返回。否则（无 ACK），丢弃该报文段并返回。If the ACK was acceptable, then signal to the user "error: connection reset", drop the segment, enter CLOSED state, delete TCB, and return. Otherwise (no ACK), drop the segment and return.

  • 三、检查安全性：

    Third, check the security:

    • 如果报文段中的安全／安全分区与 TCB 中的安全／安全分区不完全匹配，则发送复位：

      If the security/compartment in the segment does not exactly match the security/compartment in the TCB, send a reset:

      • 如果有 ACK，

        If there is an ACK,

        • <SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST><SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST>

      • 否则，

        Otherwise,

        • <SEQ=0><ACK=SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN><CTL=RST,ACK><SEQ=0><ACK=SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN><CTL=RST,ACK>
    • 如果发送了复位，则丢弃该报文段并返回。If a reset was sent, discard the segment and return.

  • 四、检查 SYN 位：

    Fourth, check the SYN bit:

    • 仅当 ACK 正常或者没有 ACK 并且该报文段不包含 RST 时才应到达此步骤。This step should be reached only if the ACK is ok, or there is no ACK, and the segment did not contain a RST.
    • 若 SYN 位置位，且安全／安全分区可接受，则设置 RCV.NXT = SEG.SEQ+1、IRS = SEG.SEQ。若带有 ACK，推进 SND.UNA 至 SEG.ACK，并从重传队列中删除由此得到确认的报文段。If the SYN bit is on and the security/compartment is acceptable, then RCV.NXT is set to SEG.SEQ+1, IRS is set to SEG.SEQ. SND.UNA should be advanced to equal SEG.ACK (if there is an ACK), and any segments on the retransmission queue that are thereby acknowledged should be removed.

    • 如果SND.UNA > ISS（我们的 SYN 已被 ACK），将连接状态更改为ESTABLISHED，形成 ACK 报文段

      If SND.UNA > ISS (our SYN has been ACKed), change the connection state to ESTABLISHED, form an ACK segment

      • <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK><SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>
    • 然后发送。可以（MAY）捎带排队待发的数据或控制信息。若收到的报文段含有数据，而后续处理本就会为这些数据产生确认，某些实现会省略此处的单独发送，从而避免额外发出一个仅确认 SYN 的报文段。若报文段还含其他控制信息或数据，继续到第 3.10.7.4 节中检查 URG 位的第六步；否则返回。and send it. Data or controls that were queued for transmission MAY be included. Some TCP implementations suppress sending this segment when the received segment contains data that will anyways generate an acknowledgment in the later processing steps, saving this extra acknowledgment of the SYN from being sent. If there are other controls or text in the segment, then continue processing at the sixth step under Section 3.10.7.4 where the URG bit is checked; otherwise, return.

    • 否则，输入 SYN-RECEIVED，形成 SYN,ACK 报文段

      Otherwise, enter SYN-RECEIVED, form a SYN,ACK segment

      • <SEQ=ISS><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=SYN,ACK><SEQ=ISS><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=SYN,ACK>

    • 并发送。设置变量：

      and send it. Set the variables:

      • SND.WND <- SEG.WND

        SND.WND <- SEG.WND

        SND.WL1 <- SEG.SEQ

        SND.WL1 <- SEG.SEQ

        SND.WL2 <- SEG.ACK

        SND.WL2 <- SEG.ACK

      若报文段还包含其他控制信息或数据，则将其排队，待进入 ESTABLISHED 状态后处理，并返回。

      If there are other controls or text in the segment, queue them for processing after the ESTABLISHED state has been reached, return.

    • 在 SYN 报文段中发送和接收应用数据是合法的，这就是上文所说的“报文段中的数据”。历史上对此存在大量错误信息和误解，某些防火墙及安全设备会将其视为可疑行为。不过，T/TCP [21] 曾使用这一功能，TCP Fast Open（TFO）[48] 也在使用，因此实现和网络设备允许这种行为十分重要。Note that it is legal to send and receive application data on SYN segments (this is the "text in the segment" mentioned above). There has been significant misinformation and misunderstanding of this topic historically. Some firewalls and security devices consider this suspicious. However, the capability was used in T/TCP [21] and is used in TCP Fast Open (TFO) [48], so is important for implementations and network devices to permit.
  • 第五，如果 SYN 或 RST 位均未设置，则丢弃该报文段并返回。Fifth, if neither of the SYN or RST bits is set, then drop the segment and return.
3.10.7.4. 其他状态

否则，

Otherwise,

  • 首先，检查序号：

    First, check sequence number:

    • SYN-RECEIVED 状态SYN-RECEIVED STATE
    • ESTABLISHED 状态ESTABLISHED STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-1 STATEFIN-WAIT-1 STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-2 STATEFIN-WAIT-2 STATE
    • CLOSE-WAIT 状态CLOSE-WAIT STATE
    • CLOSING 状态CLOSING STATE
    • LAST-ACK 状态LAST-ACK STATE

    • TIME-WAIT 状态

      TIME-WAIT STATE

      • 按序处理报文段。到达时先进行检查，以丢弃旧的重复副本；后续处理按 SEG.SEQ 顺序进行。若内容跨越新旧数据边界，只处理新的部分。Segments are processed in sequence. Initial tests on arrival are used to discard old duplicates, but further processing is done in SEG.SEQ order. If a segment's contents straddle the boundary between old and new, only the new parts are processed.
      • 一般来说，接收报文段的处理必须（MUST）实现以尽可能聚合 ACK 报文段（MUST-58）。例如，如果 TCP 端点正在处理一系列排队的报文段，则它必须 (MUST) 在发送任何 ACK 报文段之前处理所有这些报文段 (MUST-59)。In general, the processing of received segments MUST be implemented to aggregate ACK segments whenever possible (MUST-58). For example, if the TCP endpoint is processing a series of queued segments, it MUST process them all before sending any ACK segments (MUST-59).

      • 传入报文段的可接受性测试有四种情况：

        There are four cases for the acceptability test for an incoming segment:

        表 6：报文段可接受性检查
        报文段长度Segment Length接收窗口Receive Window检查条件Test
        0000SEG.SEQ = RCV.NXTSEG.SEQ = RCV.NXT
        00>0>0RCV.NXT =< SEG.SEQ < RCV.NXT+RCV.WNDRCV.NXT =< SEG.SEQ < RCV.NXT+RCV.WND
        >0>000不可接受not acceptable
        >0>0>0>0

        RCV.NXT =< SEG.SEQ < RCV.NXT+RCV.WND

        RCV.NXT =< SEG.SEQ < RCV.NXT+RCV.WND

        或

        or

        RCV.NXT =< SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN-1 < RCV.NXT+RCV.WND

        RCV.NXT =< SEG.SEQ+SEG.LEN-1 < RCV.NXT+RCV.WND

      • 在按照此处所述实施序号验证时，请注意 附录 A.2。In implementing sequence number validation as described here, please note Appendix A.2.
      • 如果 RCV.WND 为零，则不接受任何分段，但应给予特殊允许以接受有效的 ACK、URG 和 RST。If the RCV.WND is zero, no segments will be acceptable, but special allowance should be made to accept valid ACKs, URGs, and RSTs.

      • 如果传入的报文段不可接受，则应在回复中发送确认（除非设置了 RST 位，如果设置了，则丢弃该报文段并返回）：

        If an incoming segment is not acceptable, an acknowledgment should be sent in reply (unless the RST bit is set, if so drop the segment and return):

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

      • 发送确认后，删除不可接受的报文段并返回。After sending the acknowledgment, drop the unacceptable segment and return.
      • 请注意，对于 TIME-WAIT 状态，[40] 中描述了一种改进的算法，用于处理传入的 SYN 分段，该算法利用时间戳而不是依赖于此处描述的序号检查。当实现改进的算法时，上述逻辑不适用于在处于 TIME-WAIT 状态的连接上接收的带有时间戳选项的传入 SYN 分段。Note that for the TIME-WAIT state, there is an improved algorithm described in [40] for handling incoming SYN segments that utilizes timestamps rather than relying on the sequence number check described here. When the improved algorithm is implemented, the logic above is not applicable for incoming SYN segments with Timestamp Options, received on a connection in the TIME-WAIT state.
      • 在下文中，假设该报文段是从 RCV.NXT 开始并且不超出窗口的理想化报文段。人们可以通过修剪掉窗口之外的任何部分（包括 SYN 和 FIN）来定制实际的报文段来适应这一假设，并且仅在该报文段从 RCV.NXT 开始时才进一步处理。具有较高起始序号的报文段应当 (SHOULD) 保留以供以后处理 (SHLD-31)。In the following it is assumed that the segment is the idealized segment that begins at RCV.NXT and does not exceed the window. One could tailor actual segments to fit this assumption by trimming off any portions that lie outside the window (including SYN and FIN) and only processing further if the segment then begins at RCV.NXT. Segments with higher beginning sequence numbers SHOULD be held for later processing (SHLD-31).

  • 其次，检查 RST 位：

    Second, check the RST bit:

    • RFC 5961 [9]，3（文献 [9]） 部分描述了潜在的盲复位攻击和可选的缓解方法。这不提供加密保护（例如，如在 IPsec 或 TCP-AO 中），但可适用于 RFC 5961 中描述的情况。对于实现 RFC 5961 中描述的保护的堆栈，适用以下三项检查；否则，这些状态的处理将在下面进一步说明。

      RFC 5961 [9], Section 3 of [9] describes a potential blind reset attack and optional mitigation approach. This does not provide a cryptographic protection (e.g., as in IPsec or TCP-AO) but can be applicable in situations described in RFC 5961. For stacks implementing the protection described in RFC 5961, the three checks below apply; otherwise, processing for these states is indicated further below.

      1. 如果设置了 RST 位并且序号位于当前接收窗口之外，则静默删除该报文段。If the RST bit is set and the sequence number is outside the current receive window, silently drop the segment.
      2. 如果设置了 RST 位并且序号与下一个预期序号 (RCV.NXT) 完全匹配，则 TCP 端点必须 (MUST) 根据连接状态以下面规定的方式复位连接。If the RST bit is set and the sequence number exactly matches the next expected sequence number (RCV.NXT), then TCP endpoints MUST reset the connection in the manner prescribed below according to the connection state.

      3. 如果设置了 RST 位并且序号与下一个预期序列值不完全匹配，但在当前接收窗口内，TCP 端点必须 (MUST) 发送确认（质询 ACK）：

        If the RST bit is set and the sequence number does not exactly match the next expected sequence value, yet is within the current receive window, TCP endpoints MUST send an acknowledgment (challenge ACK):

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

        发送质询 ACK 后，TCP 端点必须 (MUST) 丢弃不可接受的报文段并停止进一步处理传入分组。请注意，RFC 5961 和勘误 ID 4772 [99] 包含实现中 ACK 限制的其他注意事项。

        After sending the challenge ACK, TCP endpoints MUST drop the unacceptable segment and stop processing the incoming packet further. Note that RFC 5961 and Errata ID 4772 [99] contain additional considerations for ACK throttling in an implementation.

    • SYN-RECEIVED 状态

      SYN-RECEIVED STATE

      • 如果设置了 RST 位，

        If the RST bit is set,

        • 如果此连接是通过被动 OPEN 发起的（即来自 LISTEN 状态），则将此连接返回到 LISTEN 状态并返回。无需通知用户。如果此连接是通过主动 OPEN 发起的（即来自 SYN-SENT 状态），则该连接被拒绝；向用户发出“连接被拒绝”的信号。无论哪种情况，都应当清空重传队列。并且在主动OPEN的情况下，进入CLOSED状态并删除 TCB，然后返回。If this connection was initiated with a passive OPEN (i.e., came from the LISTEN state), then return this connection to LISTEN state and return. The user need not be informed. If this connection was initiated with an active OPEN (i.e., came from SYN-SENT state), then the connection was refused; signal the user "connection refused". In either case, the retransmission queue should be flushed. And in the active OPEN case, enter the CLOSED state and delete the TCB, and return.
    • ESTABLISHED 状态ESTABLISHED STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-1 STATEFIN-WAIT-1 STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-2 STATEFIN-WAIT-2 STATE

    • CLOSE-WAIT 状态

      CLOSE-WAIT STATE

      • 如果设置了 RST 位，则任何未完成的 RECEIVE 和 SEND 应收到“复位”响应。所有报文段队列都应当被清空。用户还应当收到未经请求的一般“连接复位”信号。进入CLOSED状态，删除 TCB，然后返回。If the RST bit is set, then any outstanding RECEIVEs and SEND should receive "reset" responses. All segment queues should be flushed. Users should also receive an unsolicited general "connection reset" signal. Enter the CLOSED state, delete the TCB, and return.
    • CLOSING 状态CLOSING STATE
    • LAST-ACK 状态LAST-ACK STATE

    • TIME-WAIT 状态

      TIME-WAIT STATE

      • 如果 RST 位被置位，则进入CLOSED状态，删除 TCB，并返回。If the RST bit is set, then enter the CLOSED state, delete the TCB, and return.

  • 三、检查安全性：

    Third, check security:

    • SYN-RECEIVED 状态

      SYN-RECEIVED STATE

      • 如果报文段中的安全／安全分区与 TCB 中的安全／安全分区不完全匹配，则发送复位并返回。If the security/compartment in the segment does not exactly match the security/compartment in the TCB, then send a reset and return.
    • ESTABLISHED 状态ESTABLISHED STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-1 STATEFIN-WAIT-1 STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-2 STATEFIN-WAIT-2 STATE
    • CLOSE-WAIT 状态CLOSE-WAIT STATE
    • CLOSING 状态CLOSING STATE
    • LAST-ACK 状态LAST-ACK STATE

    • TIME-WAIT 状态

      TIME-WAIT STATE

      • 如果报文段中的安全／安全分区与 TCB 中的安全／安全分区不完全匹配，则发送复位；任何未完成的 RECEIVE 和 SEND 应收到“复位”响应。所有报文段队列都应当被清空。用户还应当收到未经请求的一般“连接复位”信号。进入CLOSED状态，删除 TCB，然后返回。If the security/compartment in the segment does not exactly match the security/compartment in the TCB, then send a reset; any outstanding RECEIVEs and SEND should receive "reset" responses. All segment queues should be flushed. Users should also receive an unsolicited general "connection reset" signal. Enter the CLOSED state, delete the TCB, and return.
    • 请注意，此检查位于序列检查之后，以防止来自具有不同安全性的这些端口号之间的旧连接的分段导致当前连接中止。Note this check is placed following the sequence check to prevent a segment from an old connection between these port numbers with a different security from causing an abort of the current connection.

  • 四、检查 SYN 位：

    Fourth, check the SYN bit:

    • SYN-RECEIVED 状态

      SYN-RECEIVED STATE

      • 如果连接是通过被动 OPEN 发起的，则将此连接返回到 LISTEN 状态并返回。否则，请按照下面的同步状态说明进行处理。If the connection was initiated with a passive OPEN, then return this connection to the LISTEN state and return. Otherwise, handle per the directions for synchronized states below.
    • ESTABLISHED 状态ESTABLISHED STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-1 STATEFIN-WAIT-1 STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-2 STATEFIN-WAIT-2 STATE
    • CLOSE-WAIT 状态CLOSE-WAIT STATE
    • CLOSING 状态CLOSING STATE
    • LAST-ACK 状态LAST-ACK STATE

    • TIME-WAIT 状态

      TIME-WAIT STATE

      • 如果在这些同步状态下设置了 SYN 位，则它可能是合法的新连接尝试（例如，在 TIME-WAIT 的情况下）、应复位连接的错误或攻击尝试的结果，如 RFC 5961 [9] 中所述。对于 TIME-WAIT 状态，如果使用时间戳选项并满足期望（根据 [40]），则可以接受新连接。对于所有其他情况，RFC 5961 提供了适用于某些情况的缓解措施，尽管也有提供加密保护的替代方案（请参阅 第 7 节）。 RFC 5961 建议在这些同步状态下，如果设置了 SYN 位，则无论序号如何，TCP 端点都必须 (MUST) 向远程对端发送“质询 ACK”：

        If the SYN bit is set in these synchronized states, it may be either a legitimate new connection attempt (e.g., in the case of TIME-WAIT), an error where the connection should be reset, or the result of an attack attempt, as described in RFC 5961 [9]. For the TIME-WAIT state, new connections can be accepted if the Timestamp Option is used and meets expectations (per [40]). For all other cases, RFC 5961 provides a mitigation with applicability to some situations, though there are also alternatives that offer cryptographic protection (see Section 7). RFC 5961 recommends that in these synchronized states, if the SYN bit is set, irrespective of the sequence number, TCP endpoints MUST send a "challenge ACK" to the remote peer:

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

      • 发送确认后，TCP 实现必须 (MUST) 丢弃不可接受的报文段并停止进一步处理。请注意，RFC 5961 和勘误 ID 4772 [99] 包含针对实现的附加 ACK 限制说明。After sending the acknowledgment, TCP implementations MUST drop the unacceptable segment and stop processing further. Note that RFC 5961 and Errata ID 4772 [99] contain additional ACK throttling notes for an implementation.
      • 对于未遵循 RFC 5961 的实现，本段说明 RFC 793 所规定的原始行为。若 SYN 位于窗口内，视为错误：发送复位；向所有未完成的 RECEIVE 和 SEND 返回“复位”响应；清空全部报文段队列；另行向用户发出总体“连接已复位”通知；进入 CLOSED 状态，删除 TCB 并返回。For implementations that do not follow RFC 5961, the original behavior described in RFC 793 follows in this paragraph. If the SYN is in the window it is an error: send a reset, any outstanding RECEIVEs and SEND should receive "reset" responses, all segment queues should be flushed, the user should also receive an unsolicited general "connection reset" signal, enter the CLOSED state, delete the TCB, and return.
      • 如果 SYN 不在窗口中，则不会到达此步骤，并且将在第一步中发送 ACK（序号检查）。If the SYN is not in the window, this step would not be reached and an ACK would have been sent in the first step (sequence number check).

  • 第五，检查 ACK 字段：

    Fifth, check the ACK field:

    • 如果 ACK 位关闭，则丢弃该报文段并返回if the ACK bit is off, drop the segment and return

    • 如果 ACK 位打开，

      if the ACK bit is on,

      • RFC 5961 第 5 节（文献 [9]） 描述了潜在的盲数据注入攻击，以及实现可以 (MAY) 选择包含的缓解措施 (MAY-12)。实现 RFC 5961 的 TCP 堆栈必须 (MUST) 添加输入检查，确保 ACK 值仅在 ((SND.UNA - MAX.SND.WND) =< SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT) 范围内时才可接受。所有其 ACK 值不满足上述条件的传入报文段必须 (MUST) 被丢弃并发回 ACK。新的状态变量 MAX.SND.WND 被定义为本地发送方从其对端接收到的最大窗口（受窗口缩放影响），或者可以被硬编码为最大允许窗口值。当 ACK 值可接受时，适用以下每个状态处理：RFC 5961 Section 5 of [9] describes a potential blind data injection attack, and mitigation that implementations MAY choose to include (MAY-12). TCP stacks that implement RFC 5961 MUST add an input check that the ACK value is acceptable only if it is in the range of ((SND.UNA - MAX.SND.WND) =< SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT). All incoming segments whose ACK value doesn't satisfy the above condition MUST be discarded and an ACK sent back. The new state variable MAX.SND.WND is defined as the largest window that the local sender has ever received from its peer (subject to window scaling) or may be hard-coded to a maximum permissible window value. When the ACK value is acceptable, the per-state processing below applies:

      • SYN-RECEIVED 状态

        SYN-RECEIVED STATE

        • 如果 SND.UNA < SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT，则进入 ESTABLISHED 状态并继续处理，并将以下变量设置为：

          If SND.UNA < SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT, then enter ESTABLISHED state and continue processing with the variables below set to:

          • SND.WND <- SEG.WNDSND.WND <- SEG.WND
          • SND.WL1 <- SEG.SEQSND.WL1 <- SEG.SEQ
          • SND.WL2 <- SEG.ACKSND.WL2 <- SEG.ACK

        • 如果分段确认不可接受，则形成复位分段

          If the segment acknowledgment is not acceptable, form a reset segment

          • <SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST><SEQ=SEG.ACK><CTL=RST>
        • 并发送。and send it.

      • ESTABLISHED 状态

        ESTABLISHED STATE

        • 如果 SND.UNA < SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT，则设置 SND.UNA <- SEG.ACK。重传队列上任何被完全确认的报文段都将被删除。用户应当收到对已发送并完全确认的缓冲区的肯定确认（即，SEND 缓冲区应返回“ok”响应）。如果 ACK 是重复的 (SEG.ACK =< SND.UNA)，则可以忽略它。如果 ACK 确认尚未发送的内容 (SEG.ACK > SND.NXT)，则发送 ACK，删除该报文段，然后返回。If SND.UNA < SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT, then set SND.UNA <- SEG.ACK. Any segments on the retransmission queue that are thereby entirely acknowledged are removed. Users should receive positive acknowledgments for buffers that have been SENT and fully acknowledged (i.e., SEND buffer should be returned with "ok" response). If the ACK is a duplicate (SEG.ACK =< SND.UNA), it can be ignored. If the ACK acks something not yet sent (SEG.ACK > SND.NXT), then send an ACK, drop the segment, and return.
        • 如果 SND.UNA =< SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT，则应更新发送窗口。如果 (SND.WL1 < SEG.SEQ 或 (SND.WL1 = SEG.SEQ 且 SND.WL2 =< SEG.ACK))，则设置 SND.WND <- SEG.WND，设置 SND.WL1 <- SEG.SEQ，并设置 SND.WL2 <- SEG.ACK。If SND.UNA =< SEG.ACK =< SND.NXT, the send window should be updated. If (SND.WL1 < SEG.SEQ or (SND.WL1 = SEG.SEQ and SND.WL2 =< SEG.ACK)), set SND.WND <- SEG.WND, set SND.WL1 <- SEG.SEQ, and set SND.WL2 <- SEG.ACK.
        • 请注意，SND.WND 是相对于 SND.UNA 的偏移量，SND.WL1 记录用于更新 SND.WND 的最后一个报文段的序号，SND.WL2 记录用于更新 SND.WND 的最后一个报文段的确认号。此处的检查可防止使用旧报文段来更新窗口。Note that SND.WND is an offset from SND.UNA, that SND.WL1 records the sequence number of the last segment used to update SND.WND, and that SND.WL2 records the acknowledgment number of the last segment used to update SND.WND. The check here prevents using old segments to update the window.

      • FIN-WAIT-1 STATE

        FIN-WAIT-1 STATE

        • 除了 ESTABLISHED 状态的处理之外，如果现在 FIN 报文段已被确认，则进入 FIN-WAIT-2 并继续在该状态下处理。In addition to the processing for the ESTABLISHED state, if the FIN segment is now acknowledged, then enter FIN-WAIT-2 and continue processing in that state.

      • FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

        FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

        • 除了ESTABLISHED状态的处理外，如果重传队列为空，用户的CLOSE可以被确认（“ok”），但不删除 TCB。In addition to the processing for the ESTABLISHED state, if the retransmission queue is empty, the user's CLOSE can be acknowledged ("ok") but do not delete the TCB.

      • CLOSE-WAIT 状态

        CLOSE-WAIT STATE

        • 执行与 ESTABLISHED 状态相同的处理。Do the same processing as for the ESTABLISHED state.

      • CLOSING 状态

        CLOSING STATE

        • 除了针对ESTABLISHED状态的处理外，如果 ACK 确认了我们的 FIN，则进入TIME-WAIT状态；否则，忽略该报文段。In addition to the processing for the ESTABLISHED state, if the ACK acknowledges our FIN, then enter the TIME-WAIT state; otherwise, ignore the segment.

      • LAST-ACK 状态

        LAST-ACK STATE

        • 唯一可以到达此状态的是对 FIN 的确认。如果我们的 FIN 现在被确认，则删除 TCB，进入 CLOSED 状态，然后返回。The only thing that can arrive in this state is an acknowledgment of our FIN. If our FIN is now acknowledged, delete the TCB, enter the CLOSED state, and return.

      • TIME-WAIT 状态

        TIME-WAIT STATE

        • 唯一可以到达此状态的是远程 FIN 的重传。确认并重新启动 2 MSL 超时。The only thing that can arrive in this state is a retransmission of the remote FIN. Acknowledge it, and restart the 2 MSL timeout.

  • 第六，检查 URG 位：

    Sixth, check the URG bit:

    • ESTABLISHED 状态ESTABLISHED STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-1 STATEFIN-WAIT-1 STATE

    • FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

      FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

      • 若 URG 位置位，设置 RCV.UP <- max(RCV.UP,SEG.UP)。如果紧急指针 RCV.UP 领先于用户已经取走的数据，则通知用户远端存在紧急数据。若已就这一连续紧急数据序列通知过用户，或用户仍处于“紧急模式”，则不重复通知。If the URG bit is set, RCV.UP <- max(RCV.UP,SEG.UP), and signal the user that the remote side has urgent data if the urgent pointer (RCV.UP) is in advance of the data consumed. If the user has already been signaled (or is still in the "urgent mode") for this continuous sequence of urgent data, do not signal the user again.
    • CLOSE-WAIT 状态CLOSE-WAIT STATE
    • CLOSING 状态CLOSING STATE
    • LAST-ACK 状态LAST-ACK STATE

    • TIME-WAIT 状态

      TIME-WAIT STATE

      • 由于已从远程端收到 FIN，因此不应发生这种情况。忽略 URG。This should not occur since a FIN has been received from the remote side. Ignore the URG.

  • 第七，处理报文段数据：

    Seventh, process the segment text:

    • ESTABLISHED 状态ESTABLISHED STATE
    • FIN-WAIT-1 STATEFIN-WAIT-1 STATE

    • FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

      FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

      • 一旦处于 ESTABLISHED 状态，就可以将报文段数据传送到用户 RECEIVE 缓冲区。报文段中的数据可以移动到缓冲区中，直到缓冲区已满或报文段为空。如果该报文段为空并带有 PUSH 标志，则当缓冲区返回时，会通知用户已收到 PUSH。Once in the ESTABLISHED state, it is possible to deliver segment data to user RECEIVE buffers. Data from segments can be moved into buffers until either the buffer is full or the segment is empty. If the segment empties and carries a PUSH flag, then the user is informed, when the buffer is returned, that a PUSH has been received.
      • 当 TCP 端点负责将数据传送给用户时，它还必须确认数据的接收。When the TCP endpoint takes responsibility for delivering the data to the user, it must also acknowledge the receipt of the data.
      • TCP 接受数据后，推进 RCV.NXT，使其越过已接受的数据，并依据当前缓冲区可用空间调整 RCV.WND。RCV.NXT 与 RCV.WND 之和不应减小。Once the TCP endpoint takes responsibility for the data, it advances RCV.NXT over the data accepted, and adjusts RCV.WND as appropriate to the current buffer availability. The total of RCV.NXT and RCV.WND should not be reduced.
      • 当有效报文段到达窗口中但不在窗口左边缘时（MAY-13），TCP 实现可以 (MAY) 发送一个 ACK 报文段来确认 RCV.NXT。A TCP implementation MAY send an ACK segment acknowledging RCV.NXT when a valid segment arrives that is in the window but not at the left window edge (MAY-13).
      • 请注意第 3.8 节中的窗口管理建议。Please note the window management suggestions in Section 3.8.

      • 发送如下形式的确认：

        Send an acknowledgment of the form:

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

        <SEQ=SND.NXT><ACK=RCV.NXT><CTL=ACK>

      • 如果可能的话，此确认应搭载在正在传输的分段上，而不会造成不当延迟。This acknowledgment should be piggybacked on a segment being transmitted if possible without incurring undue delay.
    • CLOSE-WAIT 状态CLOSE-WAIT STATE
    • CLOSING 状态CLOSING STATE
    • LAST-ACK 状态LAST-ACK STATE

    • TIME-WAIT 状态

      TIME-WAIT STATE

      • 由于已收到远端 FIN，正常情况下不会出现这种情况。忽略报文段数据。This should not occur since a FIN has been received from the remote side. Ignore the segment text.

  • 八、检查 FIN 位：

    Eighth, check the FIN bit:

    • 如果状态为 CLOSED、LISTEN 或 SYN-SENT，则不处理 FIN，因为 SEG.SEQ 无法验证；删除该报文段并返回。Do not process the FIN if the state is CLOSED, LISTEN, or SYN-SENT since the SEG.SEQ cannot be validated; drop the segment and return.

    • 若 FIN 位置位，则向用户发出“连接正在关闭”通知，并以相同消息完成所有待处理的 RECEIVE；推进 RCV.NXT，使其越过 FIN，并回送对 FIN 的确认。FIN 隐含着对尚未交付用户的所有报文段数据执行 PUSH。

      If the FIN bit is set, signal the user "connection closing" and return any pending RECEIVEs with same message, advance RCV.NXT over the FIN, and send an acknowledgment for the FIN. Note that FIN implies PUSH for any segment text not yet delivered to the user.

      • SYN-RECEIVED 状态SYN-RECEIVED STATE

      • ESTABLISHED 状态

        ESTABLISHED STATE

        • 进入CLOSE-WAIT状态。Enter the CLOSE-WAIT state.

      • FIN-WAIT-1 STATE

        FIN-WAIT-1 STATE

        • 若本端 FIN 已得到确认，也可能就是本报文段确认的，则进入 TIME-WAIT，启动 TIME-WAIT 定时器并停止其他定时器；否则进入 CLOSING 状态。If our FIN has been ACKed (perhaps in this segment), then enter TIME-WAIT, start the time-wait timer, turn off the other timers; otherwise, enter the CLOSING state.

      • FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

        FIN-WAIT-2 STATE

        • 进入TIME-WAIT状态。启动时间等待定时器，关闭其他定时器。Enter the TIME-WAIT state. Start the time-wait timer, turn off the other timers.

      • CLOSE-WAIT 状态

        CLOSE-WAIT STATE

        • 保持 CLOSE-WAIT 状态。Remain in the CLOSE-WAIT state.

      • CLOSING 状态

        CLOSING STATE

        • 保持CLOSING状态。Remain in the CLOSING state.

      • LAST-ACK 状态

        LAST-ACK STATE

        • 保持 LAST-ACK 状态。Remain in the LAST-ACK state.

      • TIME-WAIT 状态

        TIME-WAIT STATE

        • 保持 TIME-WAIT 状态。重新启动 2 MSL 时间等待超时。Remain in the TIME-WAIT state. Restart the 2 MSL time-wait timeout.
  • 然后返回。and return.

3.10.8. 超时

USER TIMEOUT

USER TIMEOUT

  • 无论处于何种状态，若用户超时到期，清空全部队列；向用户发出总体“错误：连接因用户超时而中止”的通知，并对所有未完成调用返回该错误；删除 TCB，进入 CLOSED 状态，然后返回。For any state if the user timeout expires, flush all queues, signal the user "error: connection aborted due to user timeout" in general and for any outstanding calls, delete the TCB, enter the CLOSED state, and return.

RETRANSMISSION TIMEOUT

RETRANSMISSION TIMEOUT

  • 对于任何状态，如果重传队列中的某个报文段重传超时，则重新发送重传队列前面的报文段，重新初始化重传定时器，然后返回。For any state if the retransmission timeout expires on a segment in the retransmission queue, send the segment at the front of the retransmission queue again, reinitialize the retransmission timer, and return.

TIME-WAIT TIMEOUT

TIME-WAIT TIMEOUT

  • 如果连接超时，则删除 TCB，进入CLOSED状态，然后返回。If the time-wait timeout expires on a connection, delete the TCB, enter the CLOSED state, and return.

4. 术语表

ACK
不占用序号空间的控制位（确认），表示该报文段的确认字段指定该报文段的发送方期望接收的下一个序号，从而确认收到所有先前的序号。A control bit (acknowledge) occupying no sequence space, which indicates that the acknowledgment field of this segment specifies the next sequence number the sender of this segment is expecting to receive, hence acknowledging receipt of all previous sequence numbers.
连接（connection）
由一对套接字标识的逻辑通信路径。A logical communication path identified by a pair of sockets.
数据报（datagram）
在分组交换计算机通信网络中发送的消息。A message sent in a packet-switched computer communications network.
目的地址（Destination Address）
打算接收报文段的端点的网络层地址。The network-layer address of the endpoint intended to receive a segment.
FIN
控制位（finis），占用一个序号，表示发送方不再发送占用序号空间的数据或控制。A control bit (finis) occupying one sequence number, which indicates that the sender will send no more data or control occupying sequence space.
清空（flush）
从存储（缓冲区或队列）中删除所有内容（数据或报文段）。To remove all of the contents (data or segments) from a store (buffer or queue).
分片（fragment）
一个逻辑数据单元的一部分。具体而言，互联网分片是互联网数据报的一部分。A portion of a logical unit of data. In particular, an internet fragment is a portion of an internet datagram.
首部（header）
消息、报文段、分片、分组或数据块开头的控制信息。Control information at the beginning of a message, segment, fragment, packet, or block of data.
主机（host）
一台计算机。特别是从通信网络的角度来看，消息的源或目的地。A computer. In particular, a source or destination of messages from the point of view of the communication network.
标识（Identification）
互联网协议中的一个字段。发送方赋予的标识值有助于将分片重新组装为数据报。An Internet Protocol field. This identifying value assigned by the sender aids in assembling the fragments of a datagram.
互联网地址（internet address）
网络层地址。A network-layer address.
互联网数据报（internet datagram）
互联网主机之间交换的数据单元，由数据及互联网首部组成；首部使数据报能够从源端路由到目的端。A unit of data exchanged between internet hosts, together with the internet header that allows the datagram to be routed from source to destination.
互联网分片（internet fragment）
互联网数据报的数据部分的一部分，连同一个互联网首部。A portion of the data of an internet datagram with an internet header.
IP
互联网协议。请参阅 [1] 和 [13]。Internet Protocol. See [1] and [13].
IRS
初始接收序号，即对端发送方在该连接上使用的第一个序号。The Initial Receive Sequence number. The first sequence number used by the sender on a connection.
ISN
初始序号，即连接中使用的第一个序号，可能是 ISS 或 IRS。应使其在给定时间范围内唯一，且攻击者无法预测。The Initial Sequence Number. The first sequence number used on a connection (either ISS or IRS). Selected in a way that is unique within a given period of time and is unpredictable to attackers.
ISS
初始发送序号。发送方在连接上使用的第一个序号。The Initial Send Sequence number. The first sequence number used by the sender on a connection.
左端序号（left sequence）
这是数据接收 TCP 端点要确认的下一个序号（或当前未确认的最低序号），有时称为发送窗口的左边缘。This is the next sequence number to be acknowledged by the data-receiving TCP endpoint (or the lowest currently unacknowledged sequence number) and is sometimes referred to as the left edge of the send window.
模块（module）
协议或其他过程的实现，通常以软件形式实现。An implementation, usually in software, of a protocol or other procedure.
MSL
最大报文段生存期，即 TCP 报文段在互联网络系统中可能存在的最长时间。本规范将其规定为 2 分钟。Maximum Segment Lifetime, the time a TCP segment can exist in the internetwork system. Arbitrarily defined to be 2 minutes.
字节（octet）
一个八比特字节。An eight-bit byte.
选项（Options）
一个选项字段可以包含多个选项，每个选项的长度可以是几个字节。An Option field may contain several options, and each option may be several octets in length.
分组（packet）
带有首部的数据封装，其逻辑内容不一定完整。该词通常侧重数据的物理封装，而非逻辑封装。A package of data with a header that may or may not be logically complete. More often a physical packaging than a logical packaging of data.
端口（port）
连接标识符的一部分，用于在端点处多路分离连接。The portion of a connection identifier used for demultiplexing connections at an endpoint.
进程（process）
正在执行的程序。从 TCP 端点或其他主机到主机协议的角度来看的数据源或目的地。A program in execution. A source or destination of data from the point of view of the TCP endpoint or other host-to-host protocol.
PUSH
不占用序号空间的控制位，指示该报文段包含必须推送给接收用户的数据。A control bit occupying no sequence space, indicating that this segment contains data that must be pushed through to the receiving user.
RCV.NXT
下一个接收序号receive next sequence number
RCV.UP
接收紧急指针receive urgent pointer
RCV.WND
接收窗口receive window
下一个接收序号
这是本地 TCP 端点期望接收的下一个序号。This is the next sequence number the local TCP endpoint is expecting to receive.
接收窗口
这表示本地（接收）TCP 端点愿意接收的序号。因此，本地 TCP 端点认为与 RCV.NXT 到 RCV.NXT + RCV.WND - 1 范围重叠的报文段携带可接受的数据或控制。包含完全超出此范围的序号的报文段将被视为重复或注入攻击并被丢弃。This represents the sequence numbers the local (receiving) TCP endpoint is willing to receive. Thus, the local TCP endpoint considers that segments overlapping the range RCV.NXT to RCV.NXT + RCV.WND - 1 carry acceptable data or control. Segments containing sequence numbers entirely outside this range are considered duplicates or injection attacks and discarded.
RST
控制位（复位），不占用序号空间，指示接收方应删除连接，无需进一步交互。接收方可以根据传入报文段的序号和确认字段来确定是否应当遵守复位命令或忽略它。在任何情况下，收到包含 RST 的报文段都不会产生 RST 响应。A control bit (reset), occupying no sequence space, indicating that the receiver should delete the connection without further interaction. The receiver can determine, based on the sequence number and acknowledgment fields of the incoming segment, whether it should honor the reset command or ignore it. In no case does receipt of a segment containing RST give rise to a RST in response.
SEG.ACK
报文段确认号segment acknowledgment
SEG.LEN
报文段长度segment length
SEG.SEQ
报文段序号segment sequence
SEG.UP
报文段紧急指针字段segment urgent pointer field
SEG.WND
报文段窗口字段segment window field
报文段（segment）
数据的逻辑单元。具体来说，TCP 报文段是一对 TCP 模块之间传输数据的单位。A logical unit of data. In particular, a TCP segment is the unit of data transferred between a pair of TCP modules.
报文段确认号
到达报文段的确认字段中的序号。The sequence number in the acknowledgment field of the arriving segment.
报文段长度
报文段占用的序号空间大小，包括其中所有占用序号空间的控制信息。The amount of sequence number space occupied by a segment, including any controls that occupy sequence space.
报文段序号
到达报文段的序号字段中的值。The number in the sequence field of the arriving segment.
发送序号
本地发送端将在连接中使用的下一个序号。最初从初始序号函数（ISN）中选取，此后每发送一个数据字节或占用序号的控制标志就递增。This is the next sequence number the local (sending) TCP endpoint will use on the connection. It is initially selected from an initial sequence number curve (ISN) and is incremented for each octet of data or sequenced control transmitted.
发送窗口
这表示远程（接收）TCP 端点愿意接收的序号。它是来自远程（数据接收）TCP 端点的报文段中指定的窗口字段的值。 TCP 实现可能发出的新序号的范围位于 SND.NXT 和 SND.UNA + SND.WND - 1 之间。（当然，预计会在 SND.UNA 和 SND.NXT 之间重传序号。）This represents the sequence numbers that the remote (receiving) TCP endpoint is willing to receive. It is the value of the window field specified in segments from the remote (data-receiving) TCP endpoint. The range of new sequence numbers that may be emitted by a TCP implementation lies between SND.NXT and SND.UNA + SND.WND - 1. (Retransmissions of sequence numbers between SND.UNA and SND.NXT are expected, of course.)
SND.NXT
下一个发送序号send sequence
SND.UNA
最早的未确认序号（左端序号）left sequence
SND.UP
发送紧急指针send urgent pointer
SND.WL1
上次窗口更新时的报文段序号segment sequence number at last window update
SND.WL2
上次窗口更新时的报文段确认号segment acknowledgment number at last window update
SND.WND
发送窗口send window
套接字（或套接字编号、套接字地址、套接字标识符）
专门包含端口标识符的地址，即 Internet 地址与 TCP 端口的串联。An address that specifically includes a port identifier, that is, the concatenation of an Internet Address with a TCP port.
源地址（Source Address）
发送端点的网络层地址。The network-layer address of the sending endpoint.
SYN
传入报文段中的控制位，占用一个序号，在连接发起时用于指示序号从何处开始。A control bit in the incoming segment, occupying one sequence number, used at the initiation of a connection to indicate where the sequence numbering will start.
TCB
传输控制块，记录连接状态的数据结构。Transmission control block, the data structure that records the state of a connection.
TCP
传输控制协议：一种主机到主机协议，用于在互联网络环境中进行可靠通信。Transmission Control Protocol: a host-to-host protocol for reliable communication in internetwork environments.
TOS
服务类型（Type of Service），一个已废弃的 IPv4 字段。相同的首部比特如今用于区分服务字段，其中包含 DSCP 值 [4] 和 2 位 ECN 代码点 [6]。Type of Service, an obsoleted IPv4 field. The same header bits currently are used for the Differentiated Services field [4] containing the Differentiated Services Codepoint (DSCP) value and the 2-bit ECN codepoint [6].
服务类型（Type of Service）
见“TOS”。See "TOS".
URG
控制位（紧急），不占用序号空间，用于指示只要有序号小于紧急指针指示值的数据需要消费，就通知接收用户进行紧急处理。A control bit (urgent), occupying no sequence space, used to indicate that the receiving user should be notified to do urgent processing as long as there is data to be consumed with sequence numbers less than the value indicated by the urgent pointer.
紧急指针（urgent pointer）
仅当 URG 位打开时才有意义的控制字段。该字段传达紧急指针的值，该指针指示与发送用户的紧急调用关联的数据字节。A control field meaningful only when the URG bit is on. This field communicates the value of the urgent pointer that indicates the data octet associated with the sending user's urgent call.

5. 相对于 RFC 793 的变更

本文档废弃了 RFC 793 以及更新了 793 的 RFC 6093 和 6528。在所有情况下，仅将规范性协议规范和要求纳入本文档中，而一些具有背景和基本原理的资料性说明可能没有包含在内。这些文档的信息内容对于学习和理解 TCP 仍然很有价值，并且它们是有效的信息参考，即使其规范性内容已被纳入本文件。

This document obsoletes RFC 793 as well as RFCs 6093 and 6528, which updated 793. In all cases, only the normative protocol specification and requirements have been incorporated into this document, and some informational text with background and rationale may not have been carried in. The informational content of those documents is still valuable in learning about and understanding TCP, and they are valid Informational references, even though their normative content has been incorporated into this document.

本文档的主体改编自 RFC 793 的 3（文献 [16]） 部分，标题为“功能规范”，并试图保持格式和布局尽可能接近。

The main body of this document was adapted from RFC 793's Section 3 of [16], titled "FUNCTIONAL SPECIFICATION", with an attempt to keep formatting and layout as close as possible.

已纳入已报告并已接受或保留以更新 RFC 793 的适用 RFC 勘误表集合（勘误表 ID：573 [73]、574 [74]、700 [75]、701 [76]、1283 [77]、 1561 [78], 1562 [79], 1564 [80], 1571 [81], 1572 [82], 2297 [83], 2298 [84], 2748 [85], 2749 [86]、2934 [87]、3213 [88]、3300 [89]、3301 [90]、6222 [91]）。由于其他更改，一些勘误表不适用（勘误表 ID：572 [92]、575 [93]、1565 [94]、1569 [95]、2296 [96]、3305 [97]、3602 [98])。

The collection of applicable RFC errata that have been reported and either accepted or held for an update to RFC 793 were incorporated (Errata IDs: 573 [73], 574 [74], 700 [75], 701 [76], 1283 [77], 1561 [78], 1562 [79], 1564 [80], 1571 [81], 1572 [82], 2297 [83], 2298 [84], 2748 [85], 2749 [86], 2934 [87], 3213 [88], 3300 [89], 3301 [90], 6222 [91]). Some errata were not applicable due to other changes (Errata IDs: 572 [92], 575 [93], 1565 [94], 1569 [95], 2296 [96], 3305 [97], 3602 [98]).

合并了对 RFC 1011、1122 和 6093 中描述的紧急指针规范的更改。请参阅 RFC 6093，详细讨论为什么需要进行这些更改。

Changes to the specification of the urgent pointer described in RFCs 1011, 1122, and 6093 were incorporated. See RFC 6093 for detailed discussion of why these changes were necessary.

已将 RFC 793 对 RTO 的讨论更新为引用 RFC 6298。RFC 1122 中的 RTO 说明最初替代了 RFC 793 的相应内容；不过，RFC 2988 本应同时标明更新 RFC 1122，而 RFC 2988 后来又被 RFC 6298 取代。

The discussion of the RTO from RFC 793 was updated to refer to RFC 6298. The text on the RTO in RFC 1122 originally replaced the text in RFC 793; however, RFC 2988 should have updated RFC 1122 and has subsequently been obsoleted by RFC 6298.

RFC 1011 [18] 包含许多有关 RFC 793 的注释，包括对 TCP 规范的一些必要更改。这些内容在 RFC 1122 中进行了扩展，其中包含对 RFC 793 的其他更改和澄清的集合。影响协议的规范性项目已纳入此处，但此处未包含 RFC 1122 中的一些历史上有用的实施建议和信息讨论。本文档现在是 TCP 规范而不是 RFC 793，更新了 RFC 1011，并且已合并 RFC 1011 中注明的注释。

RFC 1011 [18] contains a number of comments about RFC 793, including some needed changes to the TCP specification. These are expanded in RFC 1122, which contains a collection of other changes and clarifications to RFC 793. The normative items impacting the protocol have been incorporated here, though some historically useful implementation advice and informative discussion from RFC 1122 is not included here. The present document, which is now the TCP specification rather than RFC 793, updates RFC 1011, and the comments noted in RFC 1011 have been incorporated.

RFC 1122 不仅仅包含 TCP 要求，因此本文档不能完全废弃 RFC 1122。仅标记为“正在更新”RFC 1122；然而，应当理解它实际上废弃了 RFC 1122 中找到的所有关于 TCP 的材料。

RFC 1122 contains more than just TCP requirements, so this document can't obsolete RFC 1122 entirely. It is only marked as "updating" RFC 1122; however, it should be understood to effectively obsolete all of the material on TCP found in RFC 1122.

纳入了 RFC 6528 中更安全的初始序号生成算法。请参阅 RFC 6528，了解由此缓解的攻击的讨论，以及有关选择 PRF 算法和管理密钥数据的建议。

The more secure initial sequence number generation algorithm from RFC 6528 was incorporated. See RFC 6528 for discussion of the attacks that this mitigates, as well as advice on selecting PRF algorithms and managing secret key data.

添加了基于 RFC 6429 的注释，以明确阐明系统资源管理问题允许回收连接资源。 RFC 6429 已过时，因为它所描述的澄清已反映在此基本 TCP 规范中。

A note based on RFC 6429 was added to explicitly clarify that system resource management concerns allow connection resources to be reclaimed. RFC 6429 is obsoleted in the sense that the clarification it describes has been reflected within this base TCP specification.

拥塞控制实现的描述是根据 IETF BCP 或标准轨主题的文档集以及常见实现的当前状态添加的。

The description of congestion control implementation was added based on the set of documents that are IETF BCP or Standards Track on the topic and the current state of common implementations.

6. IANA 事项

在“传输控制协议 (TCP) 首部标志”注册表中，IANA 进行了本部分所述的多项更改。

In the "Transmission Control Protocol (TCP) Header Flags" registry, IANA has made several changes as described in this section.

RFC 3168 最初创建了此注册表，但仅使用 RFC 3168 中定义的新位填充它，忽略了之前在 RFC 793 和其他文档中描述的其他位。此后，RFC 8311 [54] 也对位 7 进行了更新。

RFC 3168 originally created this registry but only populated it with the new bits defined in RFC 3168, neglecting the other bits that had previously been described in RFC 793 and other documents. Bit 7 has since also been updated by RFC 8311 [54].

“位”列已在下面重命名为“位偏移量”列，因为它引用 图 1 中 TCP 首部的 16 位对齐视图中每个首部标志的偏移量。偏移量 0 到 3 中的位是 TCP 报文段数据偏移量字段，而不是首部标志。

The "Bit" column has been renamed below as the "Bit Offset" column because it references each header flag's offset within the 16-bit aligned view of the TCP header in Figure 1. The bits in offsets 0 through 3 are the TCP segment Data Offset field, and not header flags.

IANA 添加了“分配注释”列。

IANA has added a column for "Assignment Notes".

IANA 已指定如下所示的值。

IANA has assigned values as indicated below.

“TCP 首部标志”注册表也已移至全局“传输控制协议 (TCP) 参数”注册表 https://www.iana.org/assignments/tcp-parameters/ 下的子注册表。

The "TCP Header Flags" registry has also been moved to a subregistry under the global "Transmission Control Protocol (TCP) Parameters" registry https://www.iana.org/assignments/tcp-parameters/.

注册管理机构的注册程序仍为标准行动，但参考已更新至本文档，并且注释已删除。

The registry's Registration Procedure remains Standards Action, but the Reference has been updated to this document, and the Note has been removed.

7. 安全与隐私事项

TCP 设计仅包括提高连接和应用程序数据传输的稳健性和可靠性的基本安全功能，但没有内置加密功能来支持任何形式的机密性、身份验证或其他典型安全功能。非加密增强功能（例如，[9]）的开发是为了提高 TCP 连接对特定类型攻击的鲁棒性，但非加密增强功能的适用性和保护是有限的（例如，请参阅 第 1.1 节（文献 [9]））。应用程序通常利用下层（例如 IPsec）和上层（例如 TLS）协议来为 TCP 连接和 TCP 中承载的应用程序数据提供安全性和隐私性。还开发了基于 TCP 选项的方法，以支持一些安全功能。

The TCP design includes only rudimentary security features that improve the robustness and reliability of connections and application data transfer, but there are no built-in cryptographic capabilities to support any form of confidentiality, authentication, or other typical security functions. Non-cryptographic enhancements (e.g., [9]) have been developed to improve robustness of TCP connections to particular types of attacks, but the applicability and protections of non-cryptographic enhancements are limited (e.g., see Section 1.1 of [9]). Applications typically utilize lower-layer (e.g., IPsec) and upper-layer (e.g., TLS) protocols to provide security and privacy for TCP connections and application data carried in TCP. Methods based on TCP Options have been developed as well, to support some security capabilities.

若要为 TCP 连接提供完整的机密性、完整性保护和认证，包括保护其控制标志，IPsec 是当前唯一有效的方法。若只需完整性保护和认证，可使用 TCP 认证选项（TCP-AO）[38]，也曾有扩展提案为报文段有效载荷提供机密性。本节其他方法虽然能保护有效载荷的机密性或完整性，但对 TCP 首部只覆盖部分字段，例如 tcpcrypt [57]，或完全不覆盖，例如 TLS。TCP 的其他安全增强，如 ISN 生成和序号检查，也只能部分阻止攻击。

In order to fully provide confidentiality, integrity protection, and authentication for TCP connections (including their control flags), IPsec is the only current effective method. For integrity protection and authentication, the TCP Authentication Option (TCP-AO) [38] is available, with a proposed extension to also provide confidentiality for the segment payload. Other methods discussed in this section may provide confidentiality or integrity protection for the payload, but for the TCP header only cover either a subset of the fields (e.g., tcpcrypt [57]) or none at all (e.g., TLS). Other security features that have been added to TCP (e.g., ISN generation, sequence number checks, and others) are only capable of partially hindering attacks.

使用长期 TCP 流的应用程序很容易受到利用早期 TCP 规范 [33] 中描述的控制标志处理的攻击。 TCP-MD5 是一种常用的 TCP 选项，用于支持其中一些连接的身份验证，但存在缺陷，现已弃用。 TCP-AO 提供了保护长期 TCP 连接免受攻击的能力，并且具有优于 TCP-MD5 的属性。它不为应用程序数据或 TCP 首部提供任何隐私。

Applications using long-lived TCP flows have been vulnerable to attacks that exploit the processing of control flags described in earlier TCP specifications [33]. TCP-MD5 was a commonly implemented TCP Option to support authentication for some of these connections, but had flaws and is now deprecated. TCP-AO provides a capability to protect long-lived TCP connections from attacks and has superior properties to TCP-MD5. It does not provide any privacy for application data or for the TCP headers.

“tcpcrypt”[57] TCP 实验性扩展提供了以加密方式保护连接数据的能力。 TCP 流的元数据方面仍然可见，但应用程序流受到很好的保护。在 TCP 首部中，只有紧急指针和 FIN 标志通过 tcpcrypt 进行保护。

The "tcpcrypt" [57] experimental extension to TCP provides the ability to cryptographically protect connection data. Metadata aspects of the TCP flow are still visible, but the application stream is well protected. Within the TCP header, only the urgent pointer and FIN flag are protected through tcpcrypt.

TCP 路线图 [49] 包含有关 TCP 安全性的多个 RFC 的注释。这些 RFC 提供的许多增强功能已集成到本文档中，包括 ISN 生成、减轻盲目窗口内攻击以及改进对软错误和 ICMP 分组的处理。这些都在引用的 RFC 中进行了更详细的讨论，这些 RFC 最初描述了早期 TCP 规范所需的更改。此外，请参阅 RFC 6093 [39]，了解与紧急指针字段相关的安全注意事项的讨论，这也会阻止新应用程序使用紧急指针。

The TCP Roadmap [49] includes notes about several RFCs related to TCP security. Many of the enhancements provided by these RFCs have been integrated into the present document, including ISN generation, mitigating blind in-window attacks, and improving handling of soft errors and ICMP packets. These are all discussed in greater detail in the referenced RFCs that originally described the changes needed to earlier TCP specifications. Additionally, see RFC 6093 [39] for discussion of security considerations related to the urgent pointer field, which also discourages new applications from using the urgent pointer.

由于 TCP 通常用于批量传输流，因此可能会发生一些滥用 TCP 拥塞控制逻辑的攻击。一个例子是“ACK-division”攻击。 TCP 拥塞控制规范的更新包括适当字节计数 (ABC) [29] 等机制，可缓解这些攻击。

Since TCP is often used for bulk transfer flows, some attacks are possible that abuse the TCP congestion control logic. An example is "ACK-division" attacks. Updates that have been made to the TCP congestion control specifications include mechanisms like Appropriate Byte Counting (ABC) [29] that act as mitigations to these attacks.

其他攻击集中于耗尽 TCP 服务器的资源。示例包括 SYN 泛洪 [32] 或在无法取得进展的连接 [41] 上浪费资源。操作系统通常会针对这些攻击实施缓解措施。一些常见的防御措施还利用代理、状态防火墙和终端主机 TCP 实现之外的其他技术。

Other attacks are focused on exhausting the resources of a TCP server. Examples include SYN flooding [32] or wasting resources on non-progressing connections [41]. Operating systems commonly implement mitigations for these attacks. Some common defenses also utilize proxies, stateful firewalls, and other technologies outside the end-host TCP implementation.

RFC 8546 [56] 描述了协议的“线缆可见信息”（wire image）概念：TCP 明文首部会向路径上的节点暴露超出分组路由所严格必需的元数据，路径上的攻击者可能利用它们。从 TCP 获得的经验已应用于 QUIC [60] 等新传输协议的设计。IETF 还在 RFC 9065 [61] 中记录了可能适用于未来 TCP 扩展及其他传输协议的考虑事项，IAB 也在 RFC 8558 [58] 和 [67] 中提出建议；这些内容部分基于 TCP 及其扩展的经验。

The concept of a protocol's "wire image" is described in RFC 8546 [56], which describes how TCP's cleartext headers expose more metadata to nodes on the path than is strictly required to route the packets to their destination. On-path adversaries may be able to leverage this metadata. Lessons learned in this respect from TCP have been applied in the design of newer transports like QUIC [60]. Additionally, based partly on experiences with TCP and its extensions, there are considerations that might be applicable for future TCP extensions and other transports that the IETF has documented in RFC 9065 [61], along with IAB recommendations in RFC 8558 [58] and [67].

还有“指纹”方法可用于推断主机 TCP 实现（操作系统）版本或平台信息。这些收集了多个方面的观察结果，例如报文段中存在的选项、选项的顺序、各种条件下的具体行为、分组计时、分组大小以及由实现者确定的协议的其他方面，并且可以使用这些观察结果来识别有关主机和实现的信息。

There are also methods of "fingerprinting" that can be used to infer the host TCP implementation (operating system) version or platform information. These collect observations of several aspects, such as the options present in segments, the ordering of options, the specific behaviors in the case of various conditions, packet timing, packet sizing, and other aspects of the protocol that are left to be determined by an implementer, and can use those observations to identify information about the host and implementation.

由于 ICMP 消息处理还可以与 TCP 连接交互，因此存在针对 TCP 连接的基于 ICMP 的攻击的可能性。 RFC 5927 [100] 中讨论了这些问题以及已实施的缓解措施。

Since ICMP message processing also can interact with TCP connections, there is potential for ICMP-based attacks against TCP connections. These are discussed in RFC 5927 [100], along with mitigations that have been implemented.

附录 A. 其他实现说明

本节包含有关 TCP 实现决策的附加注释和参考，这些注释和参考目前不属于 RFC 系列的一部分，也不包含在 TCP 标准中。这些项目可以由实施者考虑，但尚未就将它们纳入标准达成共识。

This section includes additional notes and references on TCP implementation decisions that are currently not a part of the RFC series or included within the TCP standard. These items can be considered by implementers, but there was not yet a consensus to include them in the standard.

附录 A.1. IP 安全分区与优先级

IPv4 规范 [1] 在（现已废弃）服务类型 (TOS) 字段中包含优先级值。它在 [20] 中进行了修改，然后被区分服务 (Diffserv) [4] 的定义废弃。在网络层、TCP 实现和应用程序之间设置和传送 TOS 已过时，并由当前 TCP 规范中的 Diffserv 取代。

The IPv4 specification [1] includes a precedence value in the (now obsoleted) Type of Service (TOS) field. It was modified in [20] and then obsoleted by the definition of Differentiated Services (Diffserv) [4]. Setting and conveying TOS between the network layer, TCP implementation, and applications is obsolete and is replaced by Diffserv in the current TCP specification.

RFC 793 要求检查 IP 安全分区和传入 TCP 报文段的优先级，以确保连接内和应用程序请求的一致性。 IP 的每个方面都已经过时，没有对 RFC 793 进行具体更新。优先问题已由 [25]（即标准轨）修复，因此当前的 TCP 规范包括这些更改。然而，多级安全 (MLS) 系统可能使用的 IP 安全选项的状态目前在 IETF 中并不明显。

RFC 793 required checking the IP security compartment and precedence on incoming TCP segments for consistency within a connection and with application requests. Each of these aspects of IP have become outdated, without specific updates to RFC 793. The issues with precedence were fixed by [25], which is Standards Track, and so this present TCP specification includes those changes. However, the state of IP security options that may be used by Multi-Level Secure (MLS) systems is not as apparent in the IETF currently.

当传入分组不满足预期的安全分区或优先级期望时复位连接已被认为是可能的攻击向量 [63]，并且已经讨论了修改 TCP 规范以防止由于不匹配的 IP 安全分区和 Diffserv 代码点值而中止连接。

Resetting connections when incoming packets do not meet expected security compartment or precedence expectations has been recognized as a possible attack vector [63], and there has been discussion about amending the TCP specification to prevent connections from being aborted due to nonmatching IP security compartment and Diffserv codepoint values.

附录 A.1.1. 优先级

在 Diffserv 中，以前的优先级值被视为类选择器代码点，并且 Diffserv 架构中描述了兼容处理的方法。 RFC 793 和 1122 定义的 RFC TCP 规范包含的逻辑旨在让连接使用任一端点应用程序请求的最高优先级，并在整个连接中保持优先级一致。过时 TOS 中的此逻辑不适用于 Diffserv，并且不应包含在 TCP 实现中，尽管不鼓励对连接内的 Diffserv 值进行更改。有关对此的讨论，请参阅 RFC 7657（5.1（文献 [50]）、5.3（文献 [50]） 和 6（文献 [50]） 节）[50]。

In Diffserv, the former precedence values are treated as Class Selector codepoints, and methods for compatible treatment are described in the Diffserv architecture. The RFC TCP specification defined by RFCs 793 and 1122 included logic intending to have connections use the highest precedence requested by either endpoint application, and to keep the precedence consistent throughout a connection. This logic from the obsolete TOS is not applicable to Diffserv and should not be included in TCP implementations, though changes to Diffserv values within a connection are discouraged. For discussion of this, see RFC 7657 (Sections 5.1 of [50], 5.3 of [50], and 6 of [50]) [50].

TCP 中已过时的 TOS 处理规则假定连接上使用双向（或对称）优先级值，但 Diffserv 架构是不对称的。 [25]中描述了旧的 TCP 逻辑在这方面的问题，所描述的解决方案是忽略 TCP 中的 IP 优先级。由于 RFC 2873 是一个标准轨文档（尽管未标记为更新 RFC 793），因此当前的实现预计在这些条件下会很稳健。请注意，每个方向使用的 Diffserv 字段值是 TCP 和网络层之间接口的一部分，并且使用的值可以在 TCP 和应用程序之间双向指示。

The obsoleted TOS processing rules in TCP assumed bidirectional (or symmetric) precedence values used on a connection, but the Diffserv architecture is asymmetric. Problems with the old TCP logic in this regard were described in [25], and the solution described is to ignore IP precedence in TCP. Since RFC 2873 is a Standards Track document (although not marked as updating RFC 793), current implementations are expected to be robust in these conditions. Note that the Diffserv field value used in each direction is a part of the interface between TCP and the network layer, and values in use can be indicated both ways between TCP and the application.

附录 A.1.2. 多级安全（MLS）系统

RFC 791 [1] 所定义的 IP 安全选项（IPSO）及安全分区后来经 RFC 1038 细化，再由 RFC 1108 取代。商业 IP 安全选项（CIPSO）定义于 FIPS-188，该标准于 2015 年被 NIST 撤销，但仍有部分厂商和操作系统支持。RFC 1108 现已列为历史文档，RFC 791 本身却尚未通过更新移除 IP 安全选项。IPv6 也定义了类似选项，即通用架构标签 IPv6 安全选项（CALIPSO）[36]。RFC 793 的处理逻辑包含对 IP 安全／安全分区信息的使用。本文对这类信息的引用可能与多级安全（MLS）系统实现者有关；非 MLS 实现者可以忽略，互联网中的现有代码也采取这一做法。更多讨论见附录 A.1。RFC 5570 描述了一些可使用 IPSO、CIPSO 或 CALIPSO 的 MLS 网络场景；针对这些特殊情形，TCP 实现者应参考 RFC 5570 第 7.3.1 节并遵循其指导。

The IP Security Option (IPSO) and compartment defined in [1] was refined in RFC 1038, which was later obsoleted by RFC 1108. The Commercial IP Security Option (CIPSO) is defined in FIPS-188 (withdrawn by NIST in 2015) and is supported by some vendors and operating systems. RFC 1108 is now Historic, though RFC 791 itself has not been updated to remove the IP Security Option. For IPv6, a similar option (Common Architecture Label IPv6 Security Option (CALIPSO)) has been defined [36]. RFC 793 includes logic that includes the IP security/compartment information in treatment of TCP segments. References to the IP "security/compartment" in this document may be relevant for Multi-Level Secure (MLS) system implementers but can be ignored for non-MLS implementations, consistent with running code on the Internet. See Appendix A.1 for further discussion. Note that RFC 5570 describes some MLS networking scenarios where IPSO, CIPSO, or CALIPSO may be used. In these special cases, TCP implementers should see Section 7.3.1 of [36] of RFC 5570 and follow the guidance in that document.

附录 A.2. 序号验证

在某些情况下，TCP 序号验证规则可能会阻止处理 ACK 字段。这可能会导致连接问题，如 [64] 中所述，其中包括对同时打开、自连接、同时关闭和同时窗口探测条件下潜在问题的描述。该文档还描述了 TCP 规范的潜在更改，以通过扩展可接受的序号来缓解该问题。

There are cases where the TCP sequence number validation rules can prevent ACK fields from being processed. This can result in connection issues, as described in [64], which includes descriptions of potential problems in conditions of simultaneous open, self-connects, simultaneous close, and simultaneous window probes. The document also describes potential changes to the TCP specification to mitigate the issue by expanding the acceptable sequence numbers.

这些情况在互联网中的 TCP 使用中很少出现。常见操作系统采用了不同的替代缓解措施，标准尚未选择其中一种加以统一规定，但实现者仍应考虑 [64] 所述问题。

In Internet usage of TCP, these conditions rarely occur. Common operating systems include different alternative mitigations, and the standard has not been updated yet to codify one of them, but implementers should consider the problems described in [64].

附录 A.3. 对 Nagle 算法的修改

在常见的操作系统中，Nagle算法和延迟确认都是默认实现和启用的。许多具有请求-响应方式通信的应用程序都使用 TCP，其中 Nagle 算法和延迟确认的组合可能会导致应用程序性能不佳。 [68] 中描述了对 Nagle 算法的修改，它改善了这些应用程序的情况。

In common operating systems, both the Nagle algorithm and delayed acknowledgments are implemented and enabled by default. TCP is used by many applications that have a request-response style of communication, where the combination of the Nagle algorithm and delayed acknowledgments can result in poor application performance. A modification to the Nagle algorithm is described in [68] that improves the situation for these applications.

某些常见操作系统已实现这项修改，且它不会影响 TCP 互操作性。另有许多应用直接禁用 Nagle 算法，因为通常存在支持这一操作的套接字选项。TCP 标准尚未纳入此修改，但实现者可以考虑采用。

This modification is implemented in some common operating systems and does not impact TCP interoperability. Additionally, many applications simply disable Nagle since this is generally supported by a socket option. The TCP standard has not been updated to include this Nagle modification, but implementers may find it beneficial to consider.

附录 A.4. 低水位设置

某些操作系统内核的 TCP 实现提供套接字选项，用来指定缓冲区必须积累到多少字节后，套接字层才将发送数据交给 TCP（SO_SNDLOWAT），或将接收数据交给应用（SO_RCVLOWAT）。

Some operating system kernel TCP implementations include socket options that allow specifying the number of bytes in the buffer until the socket layer will pass sent data to TCP (SO_SNDLOWAT) or to the application on receiving (SO_RCVLOWAT).

此外，另一个套接字选项（TCP_NOTSENT_LOWAT）可用于控制写入队列中未发送的字节数。这可以帮助发送 TCP 应用程序避免创建大量缓冲数据（以及相应的延迟）。例如，这对于将来自多个上层流的数据复用到连接上的应用程序可能很有用，特别是当流可能是交互式/实时和批量数据传输的混合时。

In addition, another socket option (TCP_NOTSENT_LOWAT) can be used to control the amount of unsent bytes in the write queue. This can help a sending TCP application to avoid creating large amounts of buffered data (and corresponding latency). As an example, this may be useful for applications that are multiplexing data from multiple upper-level streams onto a connection, especially when streams may be a mix of interactive/real-time and bulk data transfer.

致谢

本文档主要是 RFC 793 的修订版，Jon Postel 是该文件的编辑。由于他的出色工作，它能够持续三十年才让我们觉得有必要修改。

This document is largely a revision of RFC 793, of which Jon Postel was the editor. Due to his excellent work, it was able to last for three decades before we felt the need to revise it.

Andre Oppermann 是贡献者，帮助编辑了本文档的第一个版本。

Andre Oppermann was a contributor and helped to edit the first revision of this document.
